- `07-Rendering/Retro-Mode.md` - AUDIT RETRO1 2026-09-24, Mac ("Audit this"): FIVE LENSES OVER RETRO MODE - fidelity to DFU, WebGL2, the hosts, the pins and the docs, cost - AND EVERY CONFIRMED FINDING FIXED AND PINNED OR RECORDED. HIGH: Shift-F11 under any open window quick-loaded with no prompt - the two arms that let QuickLoad through a window read F11's code alone, so the settings screen's own toggle lost the player everything since the last quicksave (`retroToggleKey` guards both). MED: the docked twin came from the settings while the rect came from the drawn bar, and the enhanced skin draws none - a 320x154 image stretched over the whole canvas; now the texture, the lens and the rect all read the drawn bar. The effects ran on display bytes where DFU runs them between UnityCG's approximate gamma pair, so posterize's first level showed 17 where DFU's screen shows 13; the present now decodes, runs DFU's arithmetic and encodes. The image and its depth stayed bound on units 0 and 1 into the next frame that draws into them - a feedback loop for any sampler reading those units (the smooth grass read unit 0). The Morrowind arms were drawn into the retro image (on the classic set they are shown after it now). The 8 MB LUT froze a frame for ~200 ms (~750 at shift 0) and was never freed: it is built 4 ms a frame with the image shown plain meanwhile, and freed with retro off. The pins had compared the port with itself: the LUT and the palette are pinned by SHA-256, the pass's GL state texture by texture on a stateful fake GL with WebGL2's constants. LOW: the `(int)` bar, replacements uncapped, the ?interior host, a panel before the first quad, a live scissor, a menu frame's rect, the depth taken at begin, the owed rect on a resized canvas, sprite texels in image pixels, the shift capped at 7 (8 is a black world) with DFU's tip, every host's frame shown at its own foot, a failed LUT, one warning a bad stored value, the program warmed. Recorded on Ledger A (RETRO1, now TEN departures): the docked bar's width and the strip's shape under the pillarbox, DFU's camera-clear quirks, what the -sky test calls sky (billboards, the far ring), the arms' lens. And the one tie the first record claimed unreproduced is not: .NET's introsort modelled, no LUT texel moves at any shift. `test/auditretro1.test.js` 30 pins, `tools/mutants/auditretro1.json` 51 dead; retro1's thirteen broken records re-aimed by content, 54 dead. Not seen on a GPU - no probe run.

Under the Enhanced Lighting lane the lane's frame is the retro size in a second slot and resolves into the image. D, enemies no blow could reach (the dungeon's foe subsystem lazily imported a lazy-only chunk a deploy deletes under an open tab; now static, a stale chunk said on screen; PCAAO's unclamped hit chance recorded for Mac's call). F, five centaurs in town (a camp pitched in a town's rect off a stale pixel read) and THE WATCH DEFENDS THE TOWN - the port's own, on by default: a monster hunting an unwanted player in town brings the watch as the player's allies after DFU's witnessed-crime countdown; the resting arrest is DFU's and stays. AUDIT DISC19 (Mac: "Do an audit on this"): the squad no longer walks away mid-melee, a blow on a defender is Assault, a monster's kill carries no kit and an incident brings three squads at most, the player's splash and shafts pass defenders by; a save the curse bug had already rewritten loads without its Silver floor and dead spells, a death-video save re-pushes the video, the exit autosave names only this character's slots, the revival restores fatigue; the swing's first-surface cast reaches the door mesh past the weapon's reach, special doors are mesh colliders, the stale-chunk notice is read, the underground candle burns white; the cite shifter sees world.js again. Mac's call on D's recorded item: Physical Combat And Armor Overhaul's hit chance is clamped to 3..97 (the mod computes the clamp and discards it; a skill-30 character had landed 0 of 2000 blows on a Vampire), a departure on Ledger A's PCO1 row.
- `06-Systems/Climates-Calories.md` - CORPSE-FOOD 2026-09-23, Mac ("Wait, youre right. It needs to be accessible with people with it on"): ONLINE, A BODY'S FOOD IS THE ROOM'S. It was minted at the tier of whoever raised the death (a cell foe's owner, a dungeon's host) and a dungeon joiner's copy never rolled any, so an Off host's dungeon fed no Casual party and a joiner who opened a body first handed the room a list without food. Its lights are the nearest four in 64 m; ids go on from the save's; "Rest here" is the fire's (refused beyond its reach, a cold tent of your own stoked, a camp's rest keeping its fire); Off's ray stops at a peer's camp, names it, and the click opens nothing. The words: a stage line only as a need worsens, a jump's lines once as it lands, the cold a lit fire answers amber, a Warm chip for Hard's warm band, one "waterskin" and a source that fills one, Rations at the Peckish line (the 250 had written a marker ahead of the clock - an online load's fresh start wiped the needs), a poor night and a blackout's morning said. The laws: a short Off wears the drink and the wet off; the Casual loan is settled only by a refill (a falling ceiling had cut it); a vampire has no thirst chip and, in Hard, no attribute drain from needs it does not have; a relay correction shifts the markers; an Off player's online absence is paused; no `offFor` left behind. Open for Mac: corpse food online follows the tier of whoever raises the death (answered the same day - CORPSE-FOOD, above). `test/survtiers3.test.js` (19), `tools/mutants/survtiers3.json` (58); the closing run 431 records over 31 lists, 430 dead and one recorded equivalent.

- `06-Systems/Climates-Calories.md` - AUDIT SURV-TIERS, THE SECOND PASS 2026-09-23, Mac ("One more audit before we merge"): MAIN MERGED IN FIRST (nine commits, five conflicts, the potion cite rotted on both sides and now pinned whole), THEN FOUR FRESH LENSES OVER THE MERGED TREE - the fixes, integration, play, records - AND THIRTEEN FINDINGS, THREE OF THEM THE FIRST PASS'S OWN FIXES. The Off-gap shift inferred Off from any gap: it forgave WORLD5's short online absence in every tier and moved a meal eaten Off days into the future - replaced by a pause the world tick applies per span while the arc is Off, and a long Off is a fresh start (WORLD5's rule for an absence). The stamina loan paid twice after a bed or a potion refilled the pool - held now to the pool's shortfall. The live cap held only at the minute, and an ale then a Drain still made a live zero - capped where the stat is read. A lit fire now answers the cold in Casual (its fifteen degrees left a snowstorm camper charged and never repaid); a jump's replayed minutes wound to the floor with the heat and the cold as with thirst (a Hard desert fast travel arrived dead); a party night mirrored in a dungeon pays its sleep; a tent used from the wagon leaves it (it duplicated); the enhanced tavern pours before six. Plus a partyrest1 regex that still could not fail, pins and mutants the first pass claimed and lacked, the note's direction (the needs BORROW), and the records' three different lists of what Hard shares. `test/survtiers.test.js` 22 -> 29, `tools/mutants/survtiers.json` 107 (all 107 dead), 463 records over 21 lists, 462 dead and the same recorded equivalent - three had first survived and were answered (a re-aim that had landed on the thirst harm's identical line, and two assertions the law's own outputs lacked once the loan's settle and the read-time cap stood behind them).
- `06-Systems/Climates-Calories.md` - AUDIT SURV-TIERS (the first pass) 2026-09-23, Mac ("Lets first do a comprehensive audit and ensure this is perfect"): FOUR LENSES - HARD, CASUAL, WIRING, TESTS AND RECORDS - AND TWENTY-ONE FINDINGS, EACH REPRODUCED BEFORE IT WAS FIXED. Casual broke its own rules in three places: a rest in a red temperature was charged faster than DFU's hour restored it (the sleeper woke more tired, and a rest until healed never ended - `stamina.duringRest`), a meal on a full stomach was charged and wasted (`wastedMeal`, and the house's one order law, `tavernOrder`, asked after the gold and before the coin), and the stamina cost did not come back as the design said - it is a LOAN now, written per need and repaid the minute that need is met. Off was an eraser: its camps were lost at a load and dropped from the relay (kept and hidden now; a peer's are seen since SURV-OFFSIGHT), its minutes were charged to the hunger when the arc came back (moved past now), a leftover meal rolled Hard's sickness, every rest was broadcast as a bed to a mirroring party, and Off was stored as a string an older build reads as ON (it is the old switch's own `false` again). Three laws both tiers share were wrong since SURV1-7 and HEARTH1: the half hour fell to the night's band, a drain was capped against the permanent stat so an ale could make a live zero, an interior's hearth made no camp's rest; and `null` rules threw, latent in the tiers' own plumbing. (The second pass, above, found this pass's Off-gap fix wrong and replaced it.) Plus a vacuous test slice, an equivalent mutant, stale words and cites. `test/survtiers.test.js` 16 -> 22, `tools/mutants/survtiers.json` 79 records (all 79 dead), 417 survival-path records re-run (416 dead and the one recorded equivalent (`to1.json`'s `direction-diagonals-inclusive`, older than this slice), none survived, none stale, none unapplied), the Features tile checked in Chromium with the committed `tools/survTierProbe.mjs`. Six questions left for Mac on the page.
- `06-Systems/Climates-Calories.md` - SURV-TIERS 2026-09-23, Mac ("adding a new tab to climates and calories thats on by default. Something that introduces mechanics, leaves some out and is overall not a punished experience for players", then "Off, Casual, Hard" and "I want this to be a smart and thorough difficulty design. No band aids"): THE SURVIVAL SWITCH IS THREE TIERS ON ONE KEY - Off, Casual (the new default) and Hard (the arc as it stood, unchanged to the number - but for the laws both tiers share that the two audits above fixed). Casual is the same world with five rules on what it costs, written out as data in `survival/difficulty.js`: stamina is the only price of neglect; a need charges only at the stages the HUD already paints red, at Hard's rates; never below half the pool, so the needs alone can never raise DFU's lethal exhaustion collapse; no gate, no second encounter ask, no stiff morning, no sickness roll, no hunt harm (each has a declared safe twin, on the same rolls), no blackout (the barkeep stops pouring before the coin changes hands). Every charging law takes its tier's rules and runs Hard when handed none; the compositions read the live tier where they already read the switch; the resting encounter roll takes a count of asks. A stored Off stays Off (SUPERSEDED IN PART by the audit above: Off is stored as the old switch's own `false`, never converted), everyone else moves to Casual - and every tier, Off included, stays the player's online. On the way: `FLOOR_FATIGUE` was a dead constant claiming a floor Hard never had; it is gone and the floor is a tier's rule. `test/survtiers.test.js` (16), `tools/mutants/survtiers.json` (43, all dead), 21 older records re-aimed, 193 survival-path records re-run, all dead.

- `06-Systems/Community-Arc.md` - JOURNAL1 2026-09-23, Addison Knox ("Player journals ... shared in-world for storytelling"): A PAGE OF A PLAYER'S JOURNAL, HELD OUT AND KEPT. The journal is DFU's notebook. A note's Share in the chronicle holds the page out to a player within the reach a talk has (a directed `page` frame with the card's routing and the chat's mute, on its own meter), or opens the letters on it. A page held out WAITS - nothing opens over the reader's game - and is read from the F-menu's "Read their page" (`ui/pageWindow.js`), where Keep files it as a note in the reader's own journal; a letter keeps the same way. The page's law is the letter's line law, moved into wire.js so the two read one. At the root: the chronicle's composer threw on a long word (DFU's wrap throws past 70 with no space, and the composer takes 200) - `breakableNote` makes the text takeable between the characters a reader counts (`systems/graphemes.js`, EMOTE1's law, one home now). Found on the way: the chronicle's Remove acted on the card's place, not the note's; the probe found a long word running out of a note's card.
- `06-Systems/Community-Arc.md` - MAIL1 2026-09-23, Addison Knox ("An in-game mail system where players can send messages to offline players (e.g. notes, contracts, invitations)"): LETTERS TO A PLAYER WHO IS AWAY. The account service keeps them (`server-account/src/letters.js`, migration 0007, `acct6`), not the relay. Letters go registered to registered, by handle; never to a guest, never to oneself, never while muted. The law (`net/letterLaw.js`) gives the chat's characters through `wire.js` `visibleText`, keeps the lines, and refuses, never cuts, a letter past its bound. There are 20 letters an hour to anyone and 5 to one reader, and a full box of 50 refuses the sender inside the INSERT. The friends panel's Letters tab (`net/mail.js` MailBox, found every 3 minutes, not pushed) holds the box, a letter and a form no repaint can wipe, and a friend's row gains a Letter button. The probe found the three acts squeezing a phone's name to a letter a line, and they wrap now.

- `06-Systems/Community-Arc.md` - INSPECT1 2026-09-23, kurkku ("a profile page that you can bring up when you're near them") and Mac ("a new enhanced UI element for the player inspect interaction. Showing their glyph, name, title, stats and worn gear"): THE PROFILE OF A PLAYER STANDING IN FRONT OF YOU. The F-menu's Inspect row opens a card (`ui/profileWindow.js`): the title and glyphs the relay read off their token, their name, and the card their own game hands over when asked - level, race and class, the eight attributes and three vitals their sheet shows, what they wear now head to foot (`net/profileCard.js`, a directed `card` frame through the cast arm's per-sender funnel, world102). - **STATUS-LIVE + NOTICE-FIT** 2026-09-22, kurkku through Mac, in one message: "minor thing: would be nice if the info panel that comes up when you press i didn't pause the game, that way you could quickly check your status while walking around" and "I think we also need the sizing of the boxes to properly adjust for the text instead of always being wide". TWO DEFECTS WITH ONE CAUSE BETWEEN THEM, and the cause is a move that was right: ENH-NOTICE1 took DFU's centre-of-screen parchment and made it a panel at the right EDGE of the screen, and carried across two properties of the parchment that do not belong to a panel at the edge. It stopped the world, and it was a fixed slab as wide as the longest thing it would ever have to say. STATUS-LIVE: the pause was a leftover of the PRESENTATION, not a law of the thing shown. DFU pauses for the Status box because DisplayStatusInfo (DaggerfallUI.cs:1615-1628) is a DaggerfallMessageBox, a window raises `PauseWhileOpen` (UserInterfaceWindow.cs:141) and the parchment is in the middle of the screen with nothing visible past it - all true, and none of it true of a panel you can see the world behind. A READOUT IS THE PLAYER'S (AUDIT-WH R8, the law the hover plaque was built on), so the box declares DFU's own field `pauseWhileOpen: false` - which `ui/windowStack.js` has read off every window in every host's slot since ROAD-B - and all four pause latches answer false for it without a single new gate. Three things follow from not pausing. NO CHAIN: AddNextMessageBox advances on a dismissal and nothing routes this box a key, so the record-22 status text, the health box and SURV5's survival advice are ONE page, blank-line separated - which is what "quickly check your status" wanted anyway. NO ClickAnywhereToClose: a click while the world runs is a swing, so the box declines it and the panel's caption names the LIVE Status binding instead of promising a gesture it does not honour. AND IT YIELDS: the Status key is a toggle, Escape closes it and does nothing else, and any action that RAISES A WINDOW takes the slot the readout is standing in first - without which the dungeon's own free-slot guards would simply have refused the character sheet, which is a key that silently does nothing, the drawn-door-that-opens-nothing defect this port has paid for four times. The host gates that consumed every key and every click on the SLOT'S TRUTHINESS now ask the PAUSE, which is the shape `dungeonContext.js`'s twin has carried since ROAD-tail and the reason `interiorPaused` was written; the dungeon frame paints a non-pausing occupant on the PLAYED frame, because its paused arm returns above the draw; and the 60% modal DIM is gated on the window being modal, since a corridor the player is walking down cannot be blacked out. ONE COMPOSER, NOT FOUR: `world.js`, `exterior.js`, worldModes' interior arm and `dungeonContext.js` each wrote the same three-line chain out by hand, which is four places to forget when the law moves - it moved today, into `ui/statusBox.js`, with its live half in `systems/statusReadout.js` (a LEAF: `ui/input.js` owns the one door every window key comes through and is already inside `ui/actionText.js`'s import ring, and reaching for the box from there put its class body in a temporal dead zone - a real crash on a real import order, found by running it). NOTICE-FIT is the same panel's width: a fixed `width: min(520px, 70vw)` became a `max-width`, so a flex item under `align-items: flex-end` is shrink-to-fit and the rows set the width, wrapping only at the cap. Measured in Chromium by `tools/noticeFitProbe.mjs`: 210 / 450 / 520 / 339 px at 1280 wide, four widths where there used to be one. THE PROBE LIED ON ITS FIRST RUN and the picture is why it was caught - it photographed panels 46px off the right edge with a live transform, because NOTICE_WATCHDOG_MS had correctly swept a panel nobody was drawing; it pumps the draw the way a host does now. test/statuslive.test.js (10), tools/mutants/statuslive1.json (33/33 dead - three survived the first draft and all three were real: two because the caption pin pushed the binding store down itself instead of driving `bindings()` and `setBindings`, and one because a `&& !_live.done` term was dead code, since every door that raises `done` forgets the box in the same statement).
- **WATER-BACK + TRAVEL-FARE + ARREST-SHIELD** 2026-09-22, three Discord reports in one pass, and two of them are the port disagreeing with itself. WATER-BACK (kurkku, "invisible water"): THE BAND-AID OUTLIVED ITS BUG BY ONE DAY. AIWATER (2026-09-20) took the water out of every SPAWNED dungeon because "a spawn's water has been reported wrong every time - shown well below the floor, in patches, reading like a no-clip glitch", and said so honestly - "rather than keep chasing the placement". WATER-D1 (2026-09-21, the next morning) chased it and CAUGHT it, and it was never the placement: both dungeon hosts called `renderer.drawWater` AFTER drawFoes returned, which is after the first screen quad, which is where the enhanced-lighting lane resolves its frame target - so the quad landed on the default framebuffer, whose depth buffer holds no world, and passed the depth test everywhere. WATER-D1's own words: "The level itself was never the defect." A spawn was never special; it was where people met the bug because it was where people were. AND THE BAND-AID ONLY COVERED TWO OF THREE DOORS: the quad build and `blockWaterLevelAt` had the skip, `waterSurfaceYAt` - the swim TOGGLE and the drowning tick - never did, so a spawned dungeon had water that could still pull a player under and drown them with no plane drawn and no fog to say why. Invisible water in the dangerous sense. LostMyLeg's caution on the report ("when water textures are activated again they clip through walls") is a memory of the pre-WATER-D1 defect and is the right thing to be careful about, so the DRAW ORDER that makes it safe is now pinned where the re-enable lives. TRAVEL-FARE (kurkku: "really long trips don't cost anything when player-controlled cautious travel is disabled ... ship travel has the cost it should"): OL2 reasoned that online there are no nights, so no night is paid - right about the nights, WRONG ABOUT THE UNIT. DFU bills the trip's HOURS, and the journey has a length online even though the clock will not advance over it; OL2 already drew that line correctly one clause later for the ship ("a crossing is a crossing"). In practice: turn Travel Options' cautious travel off, the trip becomes an instant arrival, and online it cost no time AND no gold - free teleportation anywhere in the Bay from a mod toggle, while a ship still billed, which is exactly why it read as a bug rather than a rule. THE PIN I WROTE A DAY EARLIER MEASURED THE BUG AND CALLED IT THE LAW (travelinn: "and no night is paid"), which is the lesson: a pin that faithfully encodes a design will defend it after the design is wrong. The days stay zero - that half of OL2 is untouched and it is the half players asked for. ARREST-SHIELD (Revverie: you can die during a trial online, and the game freezes): the guard arm was right about the situation and too narrow about the attacker - online the court sequence cannot pause the world, so the boxes are read standing in the street, and a town's foes hunt every player in the cell. A bandit is not a guard; neither is a spell, a fall or drowning. Dying inside the court sequence puts a death screen and a modal trial on one window, each waiting for the other, which is the freeze. The one player damage door takes a VETO, in front of the shield pool and the SetHealth(0) branch both, and the arrest flow owns the single predicate the guard arm now reads too. 4+3+3 pins, 31 mutants, 30 dead, 1 equivalent; six older pins re-aimed and five stale mutant records re-aimed.
- **SWING-SAY** 2026-09-22, Mac ("its not working on the install but works on the browser. Each time I bring this up you avoid it"): THREE REPORTS, TWO SHIPPED FIXES, AND THE REPORTS CONTINUE - so this slice ships a READOUT rather than a third guess. MAC-SWING1 found a swing bound to a key that nothing read; MAC-D1 found an action bound to NOTHING and repaired it on load. Both are in every desktop release from app-v0.1.3638 on, checked by ancestry rather than by memory. The chain reads sound end to end from the source - swingButton/swingHeld for a mouse row, swingKeyHeld polled by all four hosts for a key row, the repair at the one load door - and the gesture FIRES in a driven test at ~10 pixels of travel, in the drag's own direction. So what is left is STATE, and the state is the one asymmetry there is: the browser keeps its store in localStorage and the desktop app keeps its own FILE (app/lib/fileStorage.cjs), which survives updates and reinstalls. One player, one machine, TWO STORES - and the app's is the older one. That is the exact shape of "works in the browser, not in the install", and no amount of reading src/ can see it. WHAT CANNOT BE READ CAN STILL BE MADE TO SPEAK: one line at the moment the binding store first becomes real, naming every code SwingWeapon answers to across both dicts, the DOM button it resolves to (-1 = the drag has no button to hold), whether it is reachable without a gamepad (the pad rows are refilled on every load, so an unbound swing still LOOKS bound), the swing MODE, and the travel a swing needs in pixels. It WARNS when the drag cannot work and logs when it can, so a player reading a console for the first time can tell the two apart without knowing what any field means. THE MODE IS THE LIKELIEST ANSWER AND THE READOUT WILL SETTLE IT: MAC-D1's own note records that both earlier reporters moved to CLICK to get an attack at all, that setting lives in the app's file, and in Click a held drag fires once on the press and never again - which is "the swiping does not work" with attacks still happening. TWO MUTANTS SURVIVED THE FIRST DRAFT and both were holes in the pins rather than in the code: the pad-row case passed even when the readout ignored the secondary dict (empty list, unreachable - right by accident), and nothing drove the boot line at all. IT REPAIRS NOTHING, and a pin holds that: a fix guessed from here would be a fix aimed at a machine nobody can see. 6 pins, 7 mutants, 7 dead.
- **FREEMOUSE** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Lets also add an entirely new keybind. A mouse free that allows you to toggle the use of your mouse"): THE TOGGLE ITSELF WAS NOT MISSING, AND THAT IS THE FINDING. DFU has `Actions.ActivateCursor`, PlayerMouseLook.cs:190-198 reads it, and the port has read it since U45 - what did not exist is a key that is ONLY the mouse. ActivateCursor's default is ENTER, and online Enter is also the chat's open (chatPanel.js CHAT_OPEN_ACTION), so the one press a player reaches for to free the mouse is the same press that opens a text box. PL3 had to WORK AROUND that rather than resolve it, because DFU's binding is DFU's and the port does not get to re-key it; a second ACTION resolves it instead of arguing with it. Enter keeps DFU's meaning, `FreeMouse` is the player's own, and the two are ORed at the ONE reader (`bindCursorToggle`) so there is no second toggle to keep in step - a second binding over `_cursorActive` is exactly the bug PL3 spent a slice on, and a host-side `e.code === 'KeyY'` is AUDIT 58's (a key-literal in a host makes a rebindable row inert in both directions). THE DEFAULT IS Y AND IT IS WHAT IS LEFT RATHER THAN WHAT IS APT: the pin RUNS the elimination rather than quoting it - DFU's table spends nineteen letters, the port spends F, J and P, and the vendored mods' defaults and OFFERED CHOICES spend B, G, K, O and X, which leaves exactly one. The obvious keys are all spoken for and each for a reason worth not undoing: Alt is Sneak, Backquote the console, Tab the pixel dial, Enter the very collision this exists to escape. It is an ACTION, so a player who wants it under their thumb moves it in the pane. THE OLD SUITE NOTICED FIRST, twice: a fixture that needed "a code no default uses" had been moved to KeyY by QUICK-LOOT B4 for exactly that reason, and FreeMouse taking Y broke it - that line is a CANARY for the keymap being full, not an ordinary fixture, and it is recorded as one now (it moved to F7, which DFU's table skips). The row is APPENDED, YIELDS in the classic windows, and is drawn in the enhanced pane under its own 'Mouse' heading rather than under 'Online': the collision is an online one, but freeing the mouse is something you do to read the screen. 5 pins, 11 mutants, 11 dead; six older enumerating pins widened and one drifted Ledger citation re-resolved.
- **QUICK-LOOT-STATS** 2026-09-22, a player relayed by Mac ("i love the quick loot but can it show the stats of the items next to the quickloot window? So i dont have to pick up everything to check in my inventory if its worth keeping"): THE ARC WAS ONLY HALF MOVED. Arc A said what a pile HELD and Arc B let you take it, so "is this worth stopping for" could be answered under the crosshair while "is this better than what I am wearing" still cost a pickup, a menu and a drop - the decision the whole arc exists to move into the world was still going through a door. A player found the gap in a day. ONE ROW, NOT SIX: the panel describes the row the player has PICKED OUT, because that is the one they are deciding about and because six stat blocks under a crosshair is a window, which is the thing this arc exists to avoid - so it needs no switch of its own, it is the highlight's other half. `itemInfo.js itemStatRows` is a THIRD PRESENTER over the producers MAC-M1 already shared between the classic popup's macro pass and the enhanced detail card, so a longsword cannot hit for 1-15 under the crosshair and 3-17 in the pack. IT SHOWS WHAT THE INVENTORY SHOWS AND NOT ONE ROW MORE, and the deliberate omission is the VALUE: `itemValueOf` is one read away and "worth keeping" sounds like it is asking for one, but the card carries no value, so a value row would not be sparing the player a trip to the pack - it would be inventing a number that surface never had, and a misleading one, because what a shop pays runs through `calculateCost` with the shop's quality and the haggling in it. THE SIDE IS ARITHMETIC AND THE NUMBERS LIVE WITH THE DRESS: `statsSide` picks right, then left, then BELOW (the phone's honest answer, in the plaque's own flow rather than hanging off an edge), and the widths are imported from enhancedStyle.js rather than restated, because a layout decided against a number the CSS no longer uses is a panel half off the screen. FOUR MUTANTS SURVIVED THE FIRST DRAFT AND EVERY ONE WAS A HOLE: the two CONDITIONAL rows were only ever exercised on a weapon and a cuirass, where neither asks its question; the row guard was tested against a DOOR, which has no rows either way, rather than against a highlight belonging to another pile; and the sheet/arithmetic pin compared the CSS against the very constant the CSS is built from, so it agreed by construction - a pin asserting the wiring as it is always agrees with it. A fifth found that nothing drove the repaint guard's new term, so a resize under a still crosshair would have left the panel hanging off the edge it was laid out against; it is driven now, in worldhover.test.js beside the DOM harness. AND THEN IT WAS PHOTOGRAPHED, because "next to" is a thing about pixels: `tools/quickLootStatsProbe.mjs` draws the plaque and its panel through the real `showWorldPlaque` over the real sheet in Chromium, at three widths, walking the highlight down a pile of four. It found two faults on its first run that no pin in the suite could have failed on - a wrapped Condition value whose second line sat against the LABEL and read as a different row, and the survival SENTENCE rows inheriting that fix's right alignment and reading as a value with no name. The rows were right, the side was right, the markup was right, and the box looked wrong. 8 pins, 15 mutants, 14 dead, 1 recorded equivalent, 1 withdrawn as not naming a law; three older row-shape pins re-aimed and three stale mutant records re-aimed.
- **MENU-RELOCK** 2026-09-22, Fedu on Discord (a 1-3 second delay before the camera answers the mouse again after closing a menu), fix drafted in PR #316 and taken over here: POINTER LOCK IS ONLY GRANTED INSIDE A USER GESTURE. The browser hands `requestPointerLock` out on the keypress the player just made and refuses it anywhere else - silently, with nothing retrying - so the look stays dead until the player's NEXT gesture happens to reach a relock arm. The delay is not a timer; it is however long it takes you to press something else, which is exactly why three people reported three different durations. THE PORT ASKED A FRAME LATE: `makeLookGate` is the per-host reconciler and it runs on the FRAME, seeing the overlay slot drain and relocking on the next call - a new task, no user activation left. The fix is not a faster gate but the four hosts asking inside the key handler that closed the window, on BOTH edges, since a two-phase window like the automap closes on key-UP and that release is a gesture too. THE INCOMING PATCH WAS RIGHT ABOUT THE CAUSE AND BROKE TWO PINS ON THE WAY: it put `const hadOverlay` between `if (mode === 'interior') {` and its routeKey call, and U43's one-dispatch law is that the interior arm routes the whole table over its own ctx with NOTHING standing in between (qs7_one_dispatch, flagsweep). The read is hoisted above the mode ladder now, where one read serves both arms because only one ctx is live per mode. Its four tests were source greps that would have passed if the fix were pasted into a comment; they are replaced by pins that DRIVE the gate and measure the gap - nothing has asked at the moment of the key, one request lands a frame later - and check the call sites by SHAPE rather than by string. ONE MUTANT FOUND A HOLE IN THE NEW PIN rather than in the source: `relock` appears twice in world.js, so a whole-file match let the host seam be renamed and still passed, while `host.relock?.()` is optional-chained and a missing seam is SILENT - every relock would simply never happen. It reads the literal's slice now. 3 pins, 10 mutants, 10 dead.
- **SHIP-PORTS** 2026-09-23, Sir McMobdon on Discord ("cant access my boat anymore after the travel update", then, having turned Travel Options' port rule off, "Still dont work ... Nether has options for the boat"): A RESTRICTION NOBODY CHOSE, WITH NO SWITCH TO REACH. Roleplay & Realism's `shipPorts` module (on in the mod, so on under the vendored-defaults rule) restricts the T picker's Ship row to a port town - Hazelnut's port list with Travel Options on - and the merge switched it on for every boat owner at once. The Features home curates three of the mod's switches and this was not one, so the player could not turn it off short of the whole mod. Two fixes: `shipPorts` is curated on the home, and it ships OFF - the one recorded departure from the mod's defaults (the same reasoning as Climates & Calories staying optional: a rule the player never asked for should not take something away silently). The defaults pin names it as the exception. Scourge Barrow's "a door wont open" (same day, #general) was not the door's law at all: it is DISC19-E (`01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-24.md`) - the throne-room door stands inside the corridor piece's placement box, and the press and the swing picked that box's walk-on record instead.

B was reverted the next day by DISC16-B.

- **DUEL1** 2026-09-24, Mac ("I want to be the foundation of pvp. When inspecting a player, they should be able to send an invite to duel which then traps both players in a surrounding transparent hographic wall that keeps them from going outside of the duel space"; asked: weapons, bows and spells; "Loser drops to 1HP and both are fully healed on duel end. Add a dueling K/D to the profile menu and player inspect profile"; outdoors only; per account): co-op's one PvP door. The Inspect card's Challenge button; the challenged player's prompt and the F-menu's Accept/Decline; a three-step handshake (ask, yes, the challenger's start with the ring's centre) so nobody is shut in a ring the other side never confirmed. A 12 m ring in the world frame, held by the motor's clamp after every step (no collider mesh), its doors shut (the travel map, a journey, rest and every building or dungeon door refuse a duellist), drawn as an additive holographic cylinder for the duellists and every onlooker (the ring rides the foes frame). After a 3 s count every blow at the opponent - a swing, a shaft, a spell's harmful families - leaves as a numbered frame and the DEFENDER resolves it through the game's one formula on its own sheet (the stub held equal to the striker's entity roll for roll); a killing duel blow leaves the loser at 1 health (`spare`, the duel's doors alone), the loser's own client reports the loss naming the winner by the relay's account stamp, and both are fully healed two seconds later. The `duel` frame is world107 (and the card answer carries its account stamp); the record is migration 0008's one INSERT (acct8), shown on the main menu's account card and the Inspect card. `06-Systems/Community-Arc.md` DUEL1.
- **DISC21** 2026-09-24, three Discord reports relayed by Mac. A (Satranath: a new character's ebony dagger "broken and cannot be worn", the repairer saying "it isn't damaged"): the biography's IT items were built by hand with no condition, and Roleplay & Realism's skill kit wore the questions' dagger to 20% of nothing - they mint through createWeapon/mintCondition now, the kit mints before it wears, and a load mints a never-minted wearable by the law it missed (conditionRepair.js). B (kurkku: the dungeon exit's wagon prompt, "Clicking 'yes' ... only closes it"): Yes opened the inventory inside the prompt's own click, into the slot the prompt still held, and the open was refused - it is taken on the next dungeon frame, DFU's close-then-open order. C (Scratchie: the weapon "in the '2' slot" and "nothing is in that slot"): the weapon is the main cell, whose ready key Z reads as a 2, and it starts sheathed as classic's does - an empty quickslot press now names the key that readies a sheathed weapon. `test/disc21.test.js` (4), `tools/mutants/disc21.json` 20/20. `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md`, DISC21.
- **DISC20** 2026-09-24, Mac, five in one message. A ("Grass isnt affected by fog"): the grass program had no fog - it takes the terrain's `fogFactorAt` verbatim now, after the pixel ramp, from the fog the ground took. B ("Sometimes when music tracks switch, its very abrupt"): DFU cuts, and so did every switch - a fader under the volume fades the old song out over 1.5 s and the next in over 1 s, the latest request winning (a recorded departure, Port-Ledger A). C ("Horse and carts can be seen parked in the sky"): a peer's word carries the owner's height, and the relay's parked teams from before TERRAIN-SCALE1 stood a fifth of the ground up - a peer's parked wagon and standing horse are stood on the viewer's ground by the mod's own probes, and a pixel rebuilt under a parked team re-stands it; a crossing no longer leaves the parked wagon's box behind. D ("Lightning can be seen even when its not storming"): storm cells outside their front's core struck on - a strike lands only where its cell is drawn, and not on snow ground. E ("The weapon widget default toggle under diverse weapons should be set to off by default"): it was; a value saved before is let go once (`SWITCH_RESETS`, stamped). `test/disc20.test.js` (13), `tools/mutants/disc20.json` 34/34. `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md`, DISC20.
- **DISC18** 2026-09-24, Mac ("my characterless [character's legs] are in the ground", unsure whether it was the save): not the save - every host drew the third-person body (Morrowind's and the EOTB sprite) at `feetAt()`, the camera's MAC1 low-passed feet, which trail a climb by its vertical speed times STEP_SMOOTH_TAU: 13 cm under the ground walking up 30 degrees, 23 running, 33 running up 40, a rung on a stair, and as far over it going down. `motor.js` `bodyFeetAt()` (EV1's lerp alone) for the five body draws; the cameras keep the smoothing. `test/disc18.test.js`, `tools/mutants/disc18.json` 7/7. `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md`, DISC18.

A ("I really want to give the wisps more opacity and reduce the amount of wind wisps"): `WISP_MAX` 240 -> 120 (a calm 19 -> 10) and the wisps' alpha doubled (0.10/0.12 -> 0.20/0.24). B ("Sometimes thunder ends abruptly"): a distant storm's thunder played from a stand-in 13 m from the ear, and a panner stays put - a walk, and the floating origin's 819.2 m recentre at every map pixel crossed, left it behind (35 dB in one frame at a crossing); `play3d`'s `far` keeps its offset from the listener until the clip ends. C ("Remove the enhanced map weather enhancements entirely"): `ui/weatherLayer.js` deleted, the held map back to the bay alone (no regions, glyphs, legend or hover forecast, none of MAP-LAG's machinery, no `paintUnder` in the sheet contract); the sim's laws stand. `test/disc17.test.js`, `tools/mutants/disc17.json` 10/10. `01-Overview/Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md`, DISC17.
- **DISC16** 2026-09-24, Mac. A ("I notice my character is sunken into the ground on hills"): DFU's body is Unity's CharacterController, and a capsule on a slope rests on its rounded bottom, its feet r(1/cos - 1) over the ground beneath its centre (5 cm at 30 degrees, 15 at 45, 35 at 60). The collider's terrain floor took the ground beneath the centre as the feet. It is the capsule's rest now (`collider.js` `restFloor`). B ("Weapon widget preset needs to be defaulted on with diverse weapons and the changes we made to the values for the weapon widget reverted", then "I just want it how it was before diverse weapons"): the weapons move as before DW1. - **MAP-LAG** 2026-09-23 (REMOVED by DISC17-C with the map's weather, 2026-09-24). Mac: "the enhanced map now is very laggy after we introduced the weather changes". The weather's regions were inked on every pan and zoom frame (30-76 ms a zoom frame), its hover read a forecast on every move (17 ms), and every open read the bay again. Now the regions are a kept raster under the ink (the sheet contract's new `paintUnder`), inked as a job 6 ms a frame and again only when the view rests. The hover's forecast waits for the pointer to rest, and the last read is kept for the next open. `Field-Bugs-2026-09-23.md` MAP-LAG.
- **DW-CLIP** 2026-09-23, two Discord reports on Diverse Weapons relayed by Mac ("mod should be defaulted on and theres an issue with the sprites getting cut off"): THE PRESET'S IDLES WERE HALF OFF THE SCREEN. The clone's DoubleScaleTextures arm adds half the rect to the Offset channel on an idle so the doubled `w_` repaint, drawn into a box UpdateWeapon made twice the classic frame, lands where the classic one stood - and the port applied that shift whenever the module was on. The mod's preset turns DoubleScaleTextures AND TrueTextureSize on, and under TrueTextureSize the box is the painting's own size, not doubled: the mod's `w_` idles are full-canvas paintings (the war axe's 317x200 IS the 320x200 screen), so half a screen's shift right and down left a snippet. The shift rides the doubling now - the module without TrueTextureSize, and a `w_` hit, the one case the box is doubled; a plain hit and the classic frame were never doubled and are not shifted either. And the preset defaults ON (it was off at DW1 because a preset nobody selected is not selected; the first player to meet the mod had to go looking for the switch). Every widget bench says the preset is off explicitly (MO1's rule), since Weapon Widget's own numbers are their subject. 1 pin over the war axe whole and the three offsets, 2 mutants (38 over dw1, all dead), four pins re-aimed.
- **MODS-ONLINE-5** 2026-09-23, Mac (asked which of Roleplay & Realism's switches to force online "especially when it comes to balance", then "Yeah its the reason we forced PCAAO"): ONE RULESET PER ROOM, SAID ONCE AND APPLIED TO BOTH. MODS-ONLINE-4 forced PCAAO whole, and the reason it gave was the host's foes - but PCAAO also rewrites the player's OWN blows, and that half was forced too without saying why. RR's six combat overrides (advanced archery, weapon speed, weapon materials, the classic strength bonus, equipment damage, encumbrance) are the same kind of thing - FormulaHelper overrides on the striker's own blow, the wearer's own armor, the walker's own load - so under the precedent's real reason they take the same answer, forced to the mod's own shipped defaults (the classic bonus ships off and stays off). And one that is an exploit rather than a preference: intensive training spends four days of world time for its +4, and the shared clock refuses the days (CLOCK-REFUSAL - the prison release's lesson), so online it would be four skill points for nothing; forced OFF, which is what the mod ships. Everything of RR's that reaches nobody stays the player's - bandaging, climbing, loans, ship ports, beds, the douse, the riding movement and trample, variable training prices, the expulsion (its squad is the expelled player's own consequence). Twenty forced switches in the shelf. The menu's lock copy gained a THIRD reason (the ruleset's), because the shared note's words - "a dungeon's foes are its host's and loot changes hands" - are not why a weapon-speed rule is locked, and a lock that gives the wrong reason is a refusal nobody can read. 1 pin, 2 mutants (18 over modsonline1, all dead).
- **MODS-ONLINE-4** 2026-09-22, Mac ("So how does this all work online?" then "What about player balance?"): THREE MODS BACK TO THE ROOM, AND THE LESSON IS THE QUESTION. Three passes had asked "does it desync?" and never asked "does my switch reach another player?". The port IS owner-authoritative, so nothing freed had broken a room - but OWNERSHIP IS NOT PRIVACY, and two of the places a machine owns something are places its answer is handed to everybody else. A world room's LAYOUT FOES ARE ITS HOST'S (WORLD2), so the host's Meaner Monsters and PCAAO are not "the host's own game" - they are what the whole party fights, and a host with either off runs a softer dungeon for four people who never chose that and cannot see why. ITEMS CHANGE HANDS: a corpse's pile is granted to peers (WORLD6b-iii(c)) and a room's containers are shared (WORLD4), so Unleveled Loot's rolls do not stay with the roller, and one player rolling level-scaled drops and passing them across is two rulesets feeding one economy. Those three are the room's now - not because a room where they differ falls apart, but because a room where they differ is one player's setting spending someone else's evening. CLIMATES & CALORIES WAS RE-ASKED THE SAME QUESTION AND KEPT THE PLAYER'S, deliberately: it reaches another player only through a corpse's FOOD - meat and rations, no power and no gear - and forcing it means nobody may opt out of freezing to death, which is a live complaint (DragynDance). So are the Shield Widget, Handheld Torches, Oblivion leveling and Travel Options, which only ever make the player's own run harder or easier. Five switches forced in the whole shelf now: the two that decide the GROUND and the three that spend a stranger's evening. The menu's lock copy is SPLIT - the three are not locked for the ground's reason, and a lock that gives the wrong reason is as useless as no reason at all. 3 mutants added (16 over modsonline1, all dead), pins re-aimed in modsonline and onlinelane.
- **MODS-ONLINE-3** 2026-09-22, Mac ("What part of mod toggle for online do you not understand?"): THE TWO MOD ROWS THAT WERE NOT IN THE MOD STORE. MODS-ONLINE-2 freed the sixteen VENDORED mods and stopped, because Climates & Calories and Weapon Sheathing are carried on the PREFS shelf - the port wrote both systems itself, from Ralzar's and Greatness7's work - and the sweep walked MOD_SETTINGS. That distinction exists nowhere a player can see it: both sit in the Mods pane under their authors' names, beside sixteen rows that were all free, and both were still locked. THE ANSWER WAS NOT "it is not really a mod", it was to sweep by what a player SEES - every feature row whose kinds carry `mod`, whichever store holds its switch. Both pass the same reading the sixteen did. The survival system writes only the entity handed to it: hunger, thirst, exposure and the felt temperature are minted fresh each tick from MY climate, MY clothes and MY race onto MY entity, a camp is local (the braziers are scenery the terrain carries either way), and a shop's stock is its own - the one thing that leaves the machine is a corpse's food, minted by the KILLER, and a corpse's pile is ALREADY the owner's word granted to peers (WORLD6b-iii(c)), which is Unleveled Loot's exact shape and Unleveled Loot is the player's. Weapon Sheathing was forced "so every body a peer sees wears its blade the same way" - a claim about how MY machine DRAWS someone else, which is `peerClassSprites`'s category and has always been the player's; a scabbard stands no object, rolls nothing and never reaches the wire, and the pose's `wd` carries a peer's drawn weapon either way. THE ENTITY CLAIM IS MEASURED, not argued: the new pin runs ten hours of a snowbound night on one body and reads the other back byte-identical. One row stays forced and is NAMED rather than left to a silent list - `enhancedEnvironments`, the port's own enhanced outdoors, which carries the shared weather's evolution and wears a mod kind only because Dynamic Skies opens its knobs on that tile (Dynamic Skies itself is the player's). ONE MUTANT WAS WITHDRAWN rather than recorded equivalent: it mutated the pin's OWN assertion, and a test cannot fail a mutation of itself. 2 pins, 2 mutants added (13 over modsonline1, 36 over ws1), all dead, two older source pins re-aimed and one stale mutant record re-aimed.
- **MODS-ONLINE-2** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Is it possible to allow all mods to be toggled on and off for online?" and then, of the first answer, "So all mods can now be toggled?"): NOW THEY CAN - fifteen of sixteen entirely, and the sixteenth in every switch but the two that move the GROUND. MODS-ONLINE freed eight and left eight forced because they sounded like world state; this half is a reading of the PORT instead of the names, and it overturns one of that pass's own rulings. THE PORT IS ALREADY OWNER-AUTHORITATIVE EVERYWHERE A MOD COULD DISAGREE: a blow's damage is the STRIKER's number and the host applies it without recomputing (applyHit: "The number is a peer's word and the host trusts it"), so PCAAO's formulas were never shared; a foe's stats are minted where it SPAWNS and a peer steps a puppet, so a joiner already fights the host's foes under the host's numbers; a corpse's pile is rolled and granted by the owner's word (WORLD6b-iii(c)); and a blow is mitigated where it LANDS - damageShieldPool inside damageFoe for a foe and inside hurtPlayer for me - so the SHIELD WIDGET's block is the defender's own, which is the opposite of what MODS-ONLINE concluded from its name. Oblivion leveling is written into a character at creation, Handheld Torches is an item in my save with a light on my screen, Travel Options is my own journey. None of it crosses as a RULE; it crosses as a RESULT, which is what the wire carries. ONE THING IS NOT A RULE - IT IS THE FLOOR: Basic Roads rewrites TERRAIN HEIGHTS (terrainGen calls smoothRoadHeights over the beds), so `Enabled` and `SmoothRoads` decide where the ground IS, and a pose is a position on that ground. AND THE OLD LANE HAD THAT BACKWARDS, which is the finding worth keeping: it forced `Enabled` and left `SmoothRoads` to the player because `SmoothRoads` is a DIAL, so the heights have been diverging online since the lane was written for anyone who turned the smoothing off for the "minor extra performance" its own description offers - forcing the mod bought the room nothing while its own dial gave the floor away. The table is by KEY now. `RiversAndStreams` is deliberately out of it and that is MEASURED, not argued: the pin runs the real smoother over every tile the river and stream tables can write and reads the heights back unchanged, because SMOOTHED_TILES is the road bed and the painter lays a road before it considers water. The player-facing copy was carrying two claims that had stopped being true and both are rewritten, with the two locked road rows given their OWN reason rather than the lane's. THE SURVIVING MUTANT FOUND A SECOND HOLE rather than a missing assertion: modSettings treated anything truthy at `keys[key]` as a declaration, so every name on Object.prototype was one - `toString` read past "is not a declared switch" and a write of it coerced against a function and stored it; the same mistake in the lane would have handed `room['toString']` back as a forced setting value. All three doors take own keys now. 7 pins, 12 mutants, 12 dead, two older pin files re-aimed.
- **DEATH-BODY1** 2026-09-22, Muriel on Discord ("Using the linux appimage, if you die in the tutorial dungeon and press F11 to load, you load the game but are visually a corpse. You can act normally, but are a corpse"), kurkku ("oh yeah I've noticed the same thing... must be some bug with Eye of the Beholder") and trashBattery, who confirmed it on Arch and NAMED THE CAUSE: "zooming into first person and then back out into 3rd person fixes it. looks like loading the game after a death doesn't update the character model state." He was exactly right. `died` in player/eotbBody.js is a LATCH - raised from the live signal, it then blocks every update below and holds the frozen death clip - and the ONLY thing that lowers it is `initialize`, whose only caller is `toggle` going active, which is precisely what scrolling out of first person and back in does. AND THE PIN THAT COVERED IT DEFENDED THE BUG: eotb_audit2's death pin ticked `died: false` twelve times and asserted the table was STILL Idle, citing the IL. The pin was faithful and the MOD IS RIGHT - in Daggerfall Unity a death ends the run, so the only way back is a load, and a load rebuilds the scene and the billboard object with it; the mod CANNOT have this bug. The port revives a module-level body instead - a quickload straight back into play, the online respawn, a prison release - and not one of those is an Initialize, so the latch outlived the death that set it. THE FIX IS AT THE SIGNAL, NOT AT ANY ONE CALLER: the body follows the ENTITY, so coming back to life lowers the latch wherever the life came from and every revival route is covered at once - a load hook would have left the respawn and the prison release still frozen, and the pin refuses that shape by name. A deliberate, documented divergence from EOTB-IL inside an otherwise IL-verbatim file, which is the lesson worth keeping: a pin that faithfully encodes the reference will defend a bug the reference could never have had. 1 pin, 3 mutants, 3 dead, one older pin re-aimed.
- **MODS-ONLINE** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Is it possible to allow all mods to be toggled on and off for online?"): YES, FOR EVERY MOD THAT DRAWS ON YOUR OWN SCREEN AND NOWHERE ELSE - and the answer is a READING of what each one touches, not a guess at what it sounds like. OL1's law is that a room plays one game: what one player walks through, another walks through. AUDIT-WH R8 had already drawn the line inside it for World Tooltips - that reasoning is about the WORLD, so a mod that moves a light, stands an object, changes a roll or writes a save record is what the room has to agree on, and one that draws a readout is not - and only `Enabled` was ever forced anyway; a mod's own dials were always the player's. Seven mods join it: dynamic-skies (the sky over a weather WORLD5 already shares - this only paints it), seasons-iliac-bay (which texture archives load), weapon-widget (its motion carries no damage, timing or attack state), eye-of-the-beholder (third person and your own billboard; peers are drawn by remotePlayers either way), ambient-text, immersive-footsteps and better-ambience. Eight stay the room's. TWO CANDIDATES FAILED THE READING, which is the whole reason it has to be one: the SHIELD WIDGET sounds cosmetic and carries `hitShield(damage, item)` and a block coroutine - it is in the damage path, and two players disagreeing about whether a blow was blocked is the room disagreeing about the blow; HANDHELD TORCHES sounds like a light and is an equipped ITEM in the save with a light standing in the world. The classification is TOTAL and pinned - every vendored mod on one side with its reason, never both, never neither, so a mod added tomorrow fails until somebody decides - and the reading itself is held by execution: each player's-side module is swept for the wire, a save record or a roll. One pin's own subject short-circuited at first (the dial test took the first mod with a dial, which is now the player's, and a player's mod returns before the key is looked at); it takes a room mod's dial now. 4 pins, 6 mutants, 6 dead.
- **CAMP-GROUND + NOTICE-SPAM** 2026-09-22, Mac ("Did you tackle camping not working in the world because of flat tarren? And the enhanced notifcation spam?"): THE CAMP PROBE ASKED THE WRONG DOOR, and that - not the silent refusal CAMP-SILENT closed - is why camping was IMPOSSIBLE rather than merely mute. `collider.raycast` walks the TRIANGLE BUCKETS alone; outside, the ground is not a mesh at all, it is `heightAt`, the terrain sampler applied to the capsule and nowhere else. So a ray cast straight down from a player standing in open country hit NOTHING, campSpot answered a null ground, and campDecision refused with "There is no level ground here" - on the flattest meadow in the Iliac Bay, everywhere outdoors, for every kit. THE DOOR THAT FIXES IT IS TWO DAYS OLD AND WAS BUILT FOR THE SAME MISTAKE: `surfaceHit`, from MAC-BUG W5, Mac's own "blood doesn't work outside" - the same collider, the same wrong reading, and its note names this caller without knowing it ("a caller that reads 'nothing' as 'no surface' is right indoors and silently wrong in the whole outdoors"). Blood was moved onto it and camping was not. It answers whichever is NEARER so a camp under a walkway still finds the walkway, and an older collider keeps the bucket probe rather than falling to null; a sweep of the other downward probes found no third victim. AND THE NOTIFICATION SPAM, from the other end: a toast system that stacks one line ten times is wrong whatever posted it. The classic column SCROLLS, so a repeat costs a row and passes; the enhanced skin draws a PLATE per row (ENH-NOTICE3), so ten copies of "You are freezing" are ten plates and nothing else can be read. An identical line already at the BACK of the queue now refreshes that row's dwell instead of adding a second, keeping its id so the plate is refreshed rather than flickering out and back - the back ONLY, because "You are hit" twice with something between them is two blows and both belong on screen. DEATHLOOP2 closed the loop that was producing them; this closes the ring that was willing to draw them. 6 pins, 7 mutants, 7 dead.
- **TRAVEL-INN** 2026-09-22, DragynDance ("when you use instant travel the game ignores the option to camp or rest at an inn") and Mac ("there's a toggle to rest at inns and stuff during travel. Why doesn't it work?"): IT WORKS OFFLINE AND IS INERT ONLINE, and neither half was written down anywhere an execution could check - so it is MEASURED now, driven out of the real popup rather than argued from source, because the chain is four modules long (the popup's refresh, travel.js's per-pixel charge, the trip cost, the host's arrival minute) and reading any one of them proves nothing about the other three. Offline: 2080 minutes and 10 gold at an inn against 2420 and free camping out - the classic `(300 * thisMove) >> 8` charge for not paying for a bed, exactly as DFU has it. Online BOTH consequences collapse to zero: WORLD5 makes the clock the world's so the trip takes no world time, and OL2 pays no night because there are no nights - 0 days and 0 gold whichever way it is set. SO THE REPORT IS LITERALLY CORRECT for the instant path, and what separates it from the silent no-ops fixed beside it is that the player is TOLD: ONLINE_TRAVEL_LINE is drawn in both skins. THE TOGGLE IS NOT DEAD ONLINE and is deliberately not greyed out - it still decides the one thing that has a consequence when no time passes, whether the trip is WALKED or instant, which isPlayerControlledTravel reads. The pin holds what is LIVE as well as what is not, so a later slice that makes an online trip spend time moves the online rows while the offline rows hold: the signal to come back and decide what an inn means when the nights belong to everyone. 3 pins, 6 mutants, 6 dead.
- **DEATHLOOP2** 2026-09-22, DragynDance on Discord ("do you know why my game keeps spamming a wailing sound at me?", beside "I almost died in privateers hold due to freezing to death" and "as soon as I stepped outside my health bar started draaaaining"), and Mac: THE WAILING IS THE DEATH SOUND, ONE PER TURN OF THE LOOP. PlayerDeathSequence plays the character's own Pain3 in its CONSTRUCTOR, and every host builds a fresh DeathScreen each time death is raised - all four are already guarded against STACKING one over another, so the repetition is the LOOP turning, with the survival notices behind it. AND DEATHLOOP1 CLOSED ONLY HALF OF IT, which is the finding worth keeping: that fix ended the effect drains - poison, continuousDamage, transferHealth - and stopped, because those were what the first reporter had. The cold is not an effect at all. survival/needs.js computes the felt temperature fresh every tick from climate, month, hour, weather, what is worn, how WET it is and the race (an Argonian carries RACE_TEMP -10, the coldest of the eight), so there was nothing on the entity for endLethalDrains to find: a frozen character revived at half health, in the same weather, still soaked, with the exposure counter still full, took the same killing tick again before they could walk anywhere - and wailed again. THE LETHALITY IS THE DESIGN AND IS LEFT ALONE, and is now pinned so the next reader does not 'fix' the loop by softening the system: needs.js withholds the health floor from that one harm on purpose and says why where it does it. What the revival clears is the two STORED fields that carry the death across it, exposure and wet, exactly as it clears a poison and for the same reason - hunger, thirst and sleep debt all survive, because a revival is not a free meal, and the weather is untouched, so walking back out into a mountain night in a loincloth still kills you. 3 pins, 7 mutants added (23 dead over the file), two mutant records re-aimed.
- **CLOCK-REFUSAL + CAMP-SILENT** 2026-09-22, chasing DragynDance's batch ("camp kits don't work for me", "if I turn on instant travel my character won't camp or rest at inns"): A REFUSAL NOBODY CAN SEE, TWICE, AT TWO LAYERS. First the code's: `advanceWorldMinutes` is refused under the shared clock and answers a NUMBER either way - the shared minute when it refuses, the new one when it moves - so a caller that MEANS elapsed time gets a plausible answer back and cannot tell that nothing happened. That is not a hypothetical: DEATHLOOP1's second half, fixed hours earlier, was exactly this - the prison release asked for the sentence's days, got a number, and let a player arrested while dying out of the cell dead. The contract is left alone (every caller reads the answer as "the clock now", which is still true); `worldClockAdvances()` is added beside it and a SWEEP requires every caller to consult the refusal or be listed with a reason. THE EXEMPTION LIST IS EMPTY AND THE PIN'S OWN STALENESS CHECK IS WHY - the first draft excused scenes/shared.js and the check answered that shared.js already asks. Then the PLAYER's: every arm of the camp placement refuses with words except one, which returned false with none, so a kit whose host could not answer for the ground did nothing and said nothing - "doesn't work" was all DragynDance could tell us because it was all the game told him. Same shape as INFO1 a day earlier from the other end (a row with nothing in it). ALSO ESTABLISHED, and NOT a bug: instant travel ignoring the camp/inn toggle is OL2's documented online behaviour and the window says so in both skins ("Online: the world's clock does not wait. You arrive now, and no inn is paid") - though it is drawn only on a NON-walked trip, so immersive travel, which online is a real ride, is the half still worth chasing; resting online is not broken either (RESTX2 simulates the hours locally); and the Argonian cold is OUR number, not Ralzar's - the survival system is an original design from C&C's IL and RACE_TEMP's -10 is the coldest of the eight, which makes freezing to death in Privateer's Hold a balance call rather than a transcription error. 4 pins, 6 mutants, 5 dead and 1 recorded equivalent (the refusal is doubled - setWorldMinutes carries the same guard, so the outer early return is belt-and-braces).
- **DEATHLOOP1** 2026-09-22, two Discord reports and one law. SquidKamer: "Respawn after poison and likely disease and other things can cause a deathloop... Its basically permanent death for your character." trashBattery: "Dying while falling with guards nearby to arrest you will place you in a deathloop once your jail sentence ends. You cannot access a menu while in this mode. The only way to stop the game now is to force-kill the process." A REVIVAL HAS TO END WHAT KILLED THEM. MAC-D3 fixed the ORDER of the revival and not its CONTENT - it moved the heal to the top so no frame could see a dead player with no death screen, which closed the window between reviving and landing; the health came back and the CAUSE did not go away, so a poisoned character woke at half health and the poison emptied it again in seconds, for ever. THE LINE IS DRAWN ON RATE: poison, continuous damage and health transfer tick on the COMBAT ROUND and are cleared; a disease's HEA column falls once per CLASSIC DAY, which is days of walking at half health and enough to reach the temple that cures it, so diseases are KEPT - and they must be, because vampirism and lycanthropy are carried as `kind: 'disease'` entries, so a blanket cure would make dying on purpose the cheapest cure in the game. THE SECOND REPORT IS A HOLE UNDER AUDIT WORLD5 C9: online a served sentence refills nothing because the clock is not this player's, and C9 was right about the free heal - but withholding the refill also withheld the FLOOR, so a player already at zero when the guards took them walked out of the cell DEAD, the death watcher raised death with no screen up, and the release ran again; the death screen owns input while it is up, which is exactly why no menu would open. Offline keeps the full refill; online gets a floor that only moves health at or below zero, so it is still not the heal C9 removed. AND IT WAS NEVER ONE CALL SITE - four paths each restored health on their own (the online respawn, Privateer's Hold, an online load of a dead save, the prison release), so the revival is one function now and the pins sweep all four. The loop is DRIVEN rather than read: a pin asserting "reviveForPlay is called" would have passed against the shipped bug too, because the shipped bug called a heal as well. Carries the F11 row with it (Janome: "in the exe version, F11 is the hotkey for full screen... so instead of making the game full screen it just loaded my save") - F9/F11 are DFU's own SetupDefaults so the game's binding is correct and the SHELL gives way: Electron's `togglefullscreen` role spends F11 on Windows and Linux, and its accelerator moves to Alt+Enter, under a law that no shell accelerator may name a key the default bindings spend. 6 pins, 13 mutants, 13 dead; two older source pins and one mutant record re-aimed, their ordering law untouched.
- `10-UI/UI-Arc.md` - **CHARGEN-REFLEX** 2026-09-22, Fay on Discord on desktop 0.1.3612 ("The Continue button on the Reflex page is not clickable. Tried all the different reflex options, but I can only click the Back button"): A CHARACTER COULD NOT BE CREATED AT ALL, and the button was COVERED rather than dead. Both first guesses were wrong and are pinned as such - it is never disabled, and the flow advances fine (headless, confirm at reflexes reaches summary). `.choose` is centred at `height: 100%` and `.stagebody`'s implicit grid row is `auto`, which is max-content, so a stage taller than the pane kept its full height while `.stagebody` shrank under `flex:1; min-height:0` - and the difference spilled out of the bottom as VISIBLE overflow, under the opaque `.actionbar` laid out straight after it. `elementFromPoint` at the button's own centre answers `DIV.actionbar` at 1280x720. REFLEXES IS THE ONLY `.choose` STAGE WITH A CONTINUE (sex, class method and biography method all advance on the ANSWER) and the tallest at 748px, five answers at `min-height: 88px` - the one screen where this could strand anybody - AND IT IS GREEN AT 1920x1080, which is why it shipped. Measured rather than read, because node cannot see a painted rectangle: tools/chargenReflexProbe.mjs reads elementFromPoint at eight window sizes, five red before the fix, and SHOW_BUG=1 re-proves it - the FIRST probe could only print OK because it reverted the CSS and not the markup, and the one before that measured my own hand-written page instead of the wizard's real height chain. THE FIX IS TWO HALVES: the layout can give way (`minmax(0,1fr)` rows, `place-content: safe center` + `overflow-y: auto` - plain `center` puts half the overflow above the start edge where nothing can scroll to it), and the primary action leaves the scrolling stage - a stage DECLARES its confirm and the action bar draws it beside Back, always on screen. The three mandatory-path stages are converted (name, biography rep box, reflexes); four short card panes are named in a KNOWN list that may shrink and never grow, LEFT DELIBERATELY because this session has no ARENA2 and converting four screens that cannot be rendered risks a worse regression. 3 pins, 8 mutants, 8 dead.
- `06-Systems/Quest-Arc.md` - **AUDIT QUESTS** 2026-09-22 (Mac: "I want you to do a deep conprehensive audit and ensure everything is linked up properly with quests and such"): THE SWEEP CAME BACK CLEAN AND THE MUTANTS DID NOT. By execution: all 265 quests parse, all 265 save/restore/save identical, the registry whole, all 188 offerable rows have files with the `-` marker honoured, the journal chain wired, and every one of the 28 `hooks.world.X` the engine reads either answered by world.js's questWorld or accounted for (3 talk* layered by talkMacroHooks, 4 court* superseded by arrestFlow's own value map). AN AUDIT THAT FINDS NOTHING IS WORTH NOTHING TOMORROW, so each link is a law now - and writing them is what found what the hand sweep had not. PIN 2 ASSERTED A STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE FAILURE: it looked for a `null` in `task.actions`, and `_readTaskLines` pushes only truthy actions and pends the raw text of anything else, so it could not have failed under any mutation. Reading `pendingActionLines` instead turned up FOUR LINES IN THE CORPUS THAT MATCH NO TEMPLATE - an unknown op-code the source itself flags, a dotted place symbol DFU's own PcAt reads as `\w+`, a `location` line the source says is not understood, and the demo's juggle, whose JuggleAction is COMMENTED OUT of DFU's registrations - each checked against Interkarma/daggerfall-unity @2343305d1 and pending there too, so none is a port gap and a FIFTH now fails the pin. PIN 3'S FIXTURE WAS TOO CLEAN TO CARRY THE FAULT: it round-tripped PRISTINE quests, where a constructor default and a saved default are the same value, so deleting a restore line changed nothing; quests are DIRTIED first now and the restoring clock is deliberately different. And a round trip has a blind spot dirtying cannot cover - a field the quest never WRITES round-trips perfectly and still loses the data - so the envelope is checked for what it CAPTURED too (the mutant that dropped `addLogStep`'s time stamp proved it: the journal dates every entry by it). THE REGISTRY LAW THAT WAS ACTUALLY MISSING is DFU's ORDER - `getActionTemplate` is a first-match scan, so a slot that moves changes which template claims a line, and it is 82 FOR 82 POSITIONALLY EXACT against DFU (eight documented renames, one declared WorldUpdate guard). auditquest_patterns.test.js compares those patterns far more deeply but SKIPS without a DFU checkout, which is CI's normal state, so the order was unguarded exactly where it matters; the fixture runs everywhere. RECORDED NOT ASSERTED: scenes/exterior.js's questWorld is 16 keys behind world.js's - the dev scene reached by `?region=&loc=` with no save path, so it is not held to the shipped surface, but promoting it cannot quietly inherit the gap. 5 pins, 13 mutants, 13 dead.
- **DAEDRA2** 2026-09-22, an Opus review against DFU's source before the merge: THE GATE AND THE PAYMENT DISAGREED. The summoning flow tested `goldAmount` (coins) and paid with `deductGold` - DeductGoldAmount, which SPENDS letters of credit - where DFU tests `GetGoldAmount`, coins plus paper (PlayerEntity.cs:1313-1316), and pays with that same DeductGoldAmount. A character was turned away as too poor from a bill the very next line could have settled. THE SUMMONING IS THE WORST SEAM FOR IT: `summoningCost` is 200,000 at reputation zero, far past what anyone carries in coin, so the gate was ALWAYS the letter and the whole service was unreachable for exactly the players it is priced for. AND IT IS A CLASS, NOT A LINE. AUDIT 26 F103-F105/F178 already named this mismatch in banking - "the gate and the payment disagreed with each other" - and three seams in `worldModes` still carried it: the summoning, the shop shelf's buy, and the repair counter, each paying with `deductGold`. All three gate on `totalGoldAmount` now, and the pin is written as a RULE over the source (no price may be gated on coins alone) so a fourth seam written tomorrow is caught by the same line rather than by the next review. The behavioural pin also caught the AUTHOR's wrong guess about the payment: DFU does not break the purse open first - when the coins cannot cover the bill it spends letters for the WHOLE of it (:1331-1341), so ten coins stay ten coins and the letter carries all 200,000. 2 pins, 4 mutants, 4 dead. A WORLD6a source pin's 200-character window was widened to 500, its law (the stale-row guard precedes the splice) unchanged.
- `05-Combat/Blood-Arc.md` - **BLOOD4** 2026-09-22, Mac from a lit interior ("So it looks reallt good, blood could be a tad bit darker") and, with a shot of a room's wall, ("youll notice in the screen shot the repetition of the wall splatter. I think we should introduce more variations"): THE COLOUR AND THE SAMENESS, and the sameness had two causes where everyone would have guessed one. The colour is a PURE SCALE - the old red times 0.81 on all three channels, so the hue cannot move (G/R 0.085 against 0.086); "a tad darker" is a luminance note, and re-mixing the channels by eye would have answered a question nobody asked. THE REPETITION: a wall mark is always the `drip` kind and always near-plumb, because a run has to run downward, so it has none of the freedom the floor's marks take from the throw - four cells were four pictures, upright, for ever. Doubling the cells to eight was the obvious half. THE HALF NOBODY WOULD HAVE GUESSED is that it would not have worked: the pin written to MEASURE the result found the eight drips at IoU 0.96 against each other, because `shapeAt`'s drip had exactly two degrees of freedom and neither moved the bead, the column or the foot. Doubling a constant cannot vary a shape that does not vary, and raising the count alone would have shipped Mac his own complaint back. So the shape gained the freedoms a real run has - where the bead struck and how big, whether it ran AT ALL (a third of them never do), how far, a sideways drift that the foot follows, and a fork - and the worst overlap is 0.76 now with the covered area spanning 244 to 581 where it spanned 370 to 410. `pickCell` mirrors horizontally on top of that (16 faces a kind, and a NEW object every time, because the cells are shared by every mark already on the wall), and a run may sit up to seven degrees off plumb, either way. THE CATCH WAS THE DEPTH. The first cut took it from the run alone, which gave the bead, the foot and every pixel of a no-run cell a thickness of ZERO - the deep end inside out - and BLOOD3's own depth pin caught it in one line. A drop is a DOME: the alpha ends at a hard rim, the thickness feathers to nothing just inside it. Three of the twelve mutants SURVIVED the first run, which is how the no-run case, the dome and the lean's direction came to be pinned at all; five older blood records were re-aimed by content. 1 pin, 2 re-aimed, 12 mutants, 12 dead.
- **DAEDRA1b** 2026-09-21, Dracula/Valentin again, on the DAEDRA1 build itself ("bruh", with the box reading "the day of summoning for Peryite[srcDataUnknown]"): A RECORD IS EXPANDED ONCE, WHERE IT IS SHOWN - AND DAEDRA1 EXPANDED IT TWICE. %dat and %pcn had landed ("Today is Tirdas the 24th of Rain's Hand", "Do you, Valentin"), so the fix worked; the composition around it did not. The coven's provider got a walk AND the summoning flow wrapped that provider in a second one. Walk one carries the shared context but not the flow's %dae, and a context that cannot answer a symbol does not leave it alone - `getMacroValue` ends its ladder at `symbolStr + '[srcDataUnknown]'`, DFU's own fourth sentinel, which is correct for a FINAL pass. So walk one wrote `%dae[srcDataUnknown]`, and walk two matched the `%dae` inside that string, filled it, and left the marker standing. Neither walk is wrong by itself, which is exactly why the DAEDRA1 pins passed: they fed the walk a RAW record, and the bug lives in composing two of them. The coven keeps a RAW reader now, and every place that SHOWS a box expands it once with everything it knows - this window's own boxes there, the flow's with its prince. The new pin drives the SHIPPED shape (two walks, and it reproduces the screenshot character for character) beside the law (one walk, no sentinel of any of the four kinds), and states the ladder's marking as intended so that nobody repairs the ladder instead of the composition. 2 pins, 1 re-aimed, 9 mutants, 9 dead.
- **INFO1** 2026-09-21, kurkku on Discord ("an empty message box appears when clicking on residences in info mode", with "I assume the intended behavior is that nothing comes up at all"): A NAMELESS ROW IS NOT A ROW. Info mode on a building says its discovered displayName, and a RESIDENCE has none - House1..House4 sit outside `NAMED_BUILDING_TYPES`, so `discoverBuilding` stores `building.name ?? ''` and the say was of an empty string. DFU survives the same call because a row there is a bare TextLabel: "" measures zero and paints nothing. A row HERE is a toast with a plate behind it (ENH-NOTICE3, one per row), so an empty string is a visible empty box - which is the thing in kurkku's screenshot. The gate went into `HudText.add`, not into the one caller, for two reasons: every `say(someName)` in the port has the same hole, and AddText's LAST line files the row in the notebook's 50-slot message ring (PopupText.cs:123), so an empty row was also writing a blank line into the journal's Messages page - a second symptom of the same call, and one nobody would ever have reported. It is the skin's departure from PopupText.AddText and is written down as one. Whitespace counts as nameless; the row is queued UNTRIMMED, because this is a gate and not a formatter. A residence's click now discovers the building and says nothing, which is what was asked for (and the closed-store line cannot fire on one: its gate is `buildingType < Temple`, and a house is 17-20 against Temple's 14). 1 pin, 4 mutants, 4 dead.
- `06-Systems/Accounts-And-Cloud-Saves-Arc.md` - ACC0-ACC1f + ACC1-CI + AUDIT-ACC, OPEN 2026-09-21 (Mac: "I now want to talk about cloud storage and account creation needed for accessing online mode. We have a solid architecture built within my other repo, Fight Life, and I wondered if any of that is reusable"). THE DESIGN RECORD, THEN THREE SLICES OF IT: ACC1a the identity token (`src/net/identityToken.js`, one home both ends, and NO ALGORITHM FIELD - the version prefix IS the algorithm, so there is no `alg: none` to offer), ACC1b the account service (`server-account/`, a SECOND Worker over D1 - guests, sessions, and a session per DEVICE), ACC1c the username, password and recovery code, and ACC1-CI THE DEPLOY THAT NEEDS NOBODY. ACC1b shipped with a four-step list headed "WHAT A PERSON HAS TO DO ONCE, BY HAND", reasoning that CI can deploy code to resources but cannot create them - and Mac read it and said "The token provided allows you to take this on yourself. I am not needed at all". He was right, and the reasoning was false of the very token that has deployed the relay since SRV-N/CI: it creates a D1 database and sets a Worker secret too. `.github/workflows/account-deploy.yml` now does all four on every push to main that touches the service, each step idempotent so a failed deploy is retried by the next push rather than waiting on somebody's afternoon. THE DATABASE ID IS RESOLVED FROM CLOUDFLARE rather than committed (a sentinel in the toml, substituted in the runner, and the substitution is CHECKED - a sed that matched nothing exits 0 and fails three steps later looking like a code fault).
